- **BR-01** original_title: Projeto de Lei nº 2338/2023 – Dispõe sobre o desenvolviment… → Projeto de Lei nº 2338, de 2023 – Dispõe sobre o desenvolvi… — The number in the form both chambers use in full ('Projeto de Lei nº 2338, de 2023', as on the Chamber page) followed by the ementa verbatim from the stored text; 'nº 2338/2023' does not appear in that form on any stored page.
- **BR-01** date_last_updated: 2026-09-02 → 2024-12-10 — The last new version of the text is the Senate substitute definitively adopted on 10 Dec 2024; the 2 Sep 2026 entry was an attachment of other bills, not a new version of PL 2338/2023.
- **BR-01** secondary_url: https://www25.senado.leg.br/web/atividade/materias/-/materi… → https://www.camara.leg.br/proposicoesWeb/prop_mostrarintegr… — Points the record's second source at the stored bill text, so that the local copy holds the bill's own text; see local_copy_reasoning.
- **BR-01** summary: Comprehensive risk-based AI framework passed by the Senate:… → Sets national rules for responsible governance of AI system… — Rewritten from the stored Portuguese text (arts. 1, 6, 13, 14, 18, 19, 25, 29, 30, 42 and 45), using the bill's own terms: 'excessive risk' (risco excessivo), 'high risk' (alto risco), general-purpose and generative AI (IA de propósito geral e generativa), systemic risk (risco sistêmico), synthetic content (conteúdo sintético), serious incidents (grave incidente), the SIA and the ANPD as competent authority. Moved liability and fines to the notes.
- **BR-01** safety_topics: - accountability & liability — Six topics, each backed by a provision: risk-based classification (arts. 12-15), prohibited practices (art. 13), general-purpose / frontier AI (arts. 29-30), human oversight (arts. 6 and 8), transparency & labelling (art. 19), incident reporting (art. 42). Dropped 'accountability & liability': the record had seven topics, one more than allowed, and the liability chapter mostly keeps the existing Consumer Defence Code and Civil Code regimes (arts. 35-36); the liability and fine provisions are now in the notes.
- **BR-01** notes: Presented in the Senate 3 May 2023; Senate plenary approved… → Presented in the Federal Senate by Sen. Rodrigo Pacheco on … — Rewritten from the stored pages and text: adds the CTIA approval, the Senate's final adoption and the bills declared prejudicados, the autógrafo of 31 Jan 2025, the Ofício and art. 65 review, the phased entry into force of art. 80, the liability and fine provisions, the Chamber's distribution, Special Committee and priority regime, the 2026 attachments, the absence of a Chamber report and the version the English translation renders. Removed the press reports on the timing of a vote and the remark that Chamber amendments send the bill back to the Senate, which no stored source shows, and the sentence on the excluded PBIA, which was about the research, not the bill.
- **BR-01** english_version: - → {"kind": "third-party translation", "url": "https://campost… — The only English text found: a Google machine translation of the Senate's revised text of 19 Dec 2024, published by Campos Thomaz Advogados (third-party); stored in this run.
- **BR-01** verification.level: A → B — level computed from the evidence by tools/pipeline.py
- **US-IL-01** re-verified, no change — pipeline run 2026-10-16-refresh-ids-br-01-us-il-01 (refresh), candidate US-IL-01

## 2026-10-14 · [2026-10-14-refresh-translation-fixes](runs/2026-10-14-refresh-translation-fixes/report.md)

changed 163

- **CN-02** summary: Regulates 'deep synthesis' services, meaning deepfakes and … → Regulates internet information services that use 'deep synt… — Duties now follow the Chinese text. Identity verification (Art. 9), review of inputs and outputs (Art. 10) and labelling (Arts. 16 and 17) bind providers only; the old wording gave them to providers and technical supporters. Security assessments of biometric-editing tools (Art. 15, para. 2, item 1) bind both. Algorithm filing (Art. 19) applies only to providers with public-opinion attributes or social-mobilisation capacity. 'Deepfakes' (not in the text) replaced by the Art. 23 definition of deep synthesis technology; 'could confuse or mislead' rendered as 'may cause public confusion or misidentification' (可能导致公众混淆或者误认, Art. 17).
- **CN-02** notes: Adopted at the CAC office meeting of 3 Nov 2022, signed 25 … → Adopted at the CAC office meeting of 3 Nov 2022, signed 25 … — Removed the remark on the secondary URL (now in verification.note) and 'Still in force as of Oct 2026' (the status field carries it). Added that technical supporters file by reference (参照, Art. 19, para. 2). Named the related records CN-09 and CN-08.
- **CN-07** original_title: 人工智能安全治理框架（1.0版） → 人工智能安全治理框架（V1.0） — Return round (14 Oct 2026): '人工智能安全治理框架（1.0版）' was not verbatim; the framework's own title block reads 人工智能安全治理框架 （V1.0）.
- **CN-07** summary: China's first national AI safety governance framework. Its … → Version 1.0 of the AI Safety Governance Framework, issued b… — Return round (14 Oct 2026): rewritten from the framework's Chinese text, now stored, with the four principles in TC260's own English wording (1.1 'Be inclusive and prudent to ensure safety' for 包容审慎、确保安全, and so on). 'Guidelines for developers, providers and users' is now supported (section 6: model and algorithm developers, service providers, users in key areas, the public). Added the dual-use weapons risk (3.2.2(c)) and loss of control (3.2.4(c)); 5.1 分类分级管理 rendered 'tiered management' after TC260's 'tiered and category-based management'.
- **CN-07** safety_topics: + catastrophic risk, + incident reporting — Return round (14 Oct 2026): added 'catastrophic risk' (3.2.2(c) nuclear, biological and chemical weapons; 3.2.4(c) AI becoming uncontrollable) and 'incident reporting' (5.7 risk and threat information sharing and emergency response; 6.2(h) providers report safety incidents and vulnerabilities to the competent authorities).
- **CN-07** notes: Released at the main forum of the 2024 National Cybersecuri… → Released at the main forum of the 2024 National Cybersecuri… — Return round (14 Oct 2026): says TC260 published both language versions and that version 2.0 was built on this one (stored notice).
- **CN-07** english_version: - → {"kind": "authentic", "url": "https://www.cac.gov.cn/cms/pu… — Return round (14 Oct 2026): set to TC260's English text, linked from the CAC notice and now stored. Kind 'authentic': the issuer published it under its own name with the same title block (V1.0); 'government translation' would also be defensible, since the Chinese is evidently the drafting text.
- **CN-09** summary: Requires explicit (user-visible) labels and implicit labels… → Sets rules for labelling AI-generated and synthesised conte… — The explicit-label duty is now tied, as in Art. 4, to services under Art. 17(1) of the Deep Synthesis Provisions; 'user-visible' became 'that users can clearly perceive' (Art. 3, 明显感知). Platform duties now cover content that is labelled, declared or suspected as generated (Art. 6, items 1 to 3). 'App stores' became 'app distribution platforms' (互联网应用程序分发平台), which verify labelling materials (Art. 7). 'Removing, altering' became 'deleting, tampering with' (删除、篡改, Art. 10).
- **CN-09** notes: Signed 7 Mar 2025 and published 14 Mar 2025, together with … → Signed 7 Mar 2025 and published 14 Mar 2025; effective 1 Se… — 'Requests for unlabelled output' corrected: Art. 9 allows content without explicit labels (不含显式标识), and implicit labels (Art. 5) still apply. 'Published together with GB 45438-2025' replaced by the Art. 11 reference and the shared effective date, because the stored text does not name the standard. Dropped the years of the three earlier rules, which the stored text does not give.
- **CN-09** english_version: - → {"kind": "third-party translation", "url": "https://www.chi… — Return round (14 Oct 2026): set to China Law Translate's translation, now stored ('By China Law Translate on 2025/03/14', title 'Measures for Labeling of AI-Generated Synthetic Content'). The findings and stored files show no English version by a public body.
- **CN-11** original_title: - → 人工智能全球治理行动计划 — Return round (14 Oct 2026): filled from the stored Chinese MFA page (《人工智能全球治理行动计划》).
- **CN-11** summary: China's action plan for international AI governance. On saf… → China's action plan for international AI governance, in 13 … — The plan says 'We need to explore' categorised and tiered management and traceability management systems (item 10); the summary now says it proposes exploring them instead of calling for them outright. Kept the plan's own terms ('technical safety guidelines for open-source communities', 'assessment of the safety ... conducted regularly'). Added the item count (13). Return round (14 Oct 2026): checked against the Chinese text: 探索 ('explore') governs 分类分级管理 and 人工智能服务可追溯管理制度 (item 10), as the summary says; 推进威胁信息共享和应急处置机制建设 is now 'mechanisms for threat information sharing and emergency response'.
- **CN-11** notes: Released 26 Jul 2025 at the 2025 World Artificial Intellige… → Released 26 Jul 2025 at the 2025 World Artificial Intellige… — Removed the remark that the Chinese title was not checked (now in verification.note) and 'in Shanghai' (in no stored file).
- **CN-11** scope_note: - → A 13-item action plan on international AI governance and de… — pipeline run 2026-10-14-refresh-translation-fixes (refresh), candidate CN-11
- **CN-12** summary: China's current flagship AI policy, focused mainly on deplo… → State Council opinions on the 'AI+' action, mainly about in… — Focus areas corrected to the six the text names (科技、产业、消费、民生、治理、全球合作); 民生 is 'people's livelihood', not 'public services'. Dropped the evaluation 'China's current flagship AI policy' and the 'dedicated section' framing; 'improving security assessment and filing' became 'optimising' (优化). Added the targets for 2027, 2030 and 2035.
- **CN-12** notes: Signed 21 Aug 2025 and published 27 Aug 2025. The NDRC coor… → Signed 21 Aug 2025 and released on 26 Aug 2025. The NDRC co… — Release date corrected from 27 Aug (the CAC re-post) to 26 Aug 2025 (the MIIT re-post of the gov.cn text, 17:57). Added the categorised and graded approach (分类分级) of item (14) and the safety-related sentences of items (6)2 and (4)1.
- **CN-12** scope_note: A national AI development policy in which safety is a minor… → A State Council policy on integrating AI across the economy… — 'About one-fifth' corrected: items (13) and (14) are about 330 of about 5,200 characters of body text (roughly 6 percent), and item (13) is mostly about state-capital oversight and financial support.
- **CN-12** english_version: - → {"kind": "third-party translation", "url": "https://cset.ge… — Return round (14 Oct 2026): set to CSET's translation, now stored (translator Etcetera Language Group, Inc., edited by CSET's translation manager). The findings show no government English text.
- **CN-13** title: AI Safety Governance Framework (Version 2.0) → AI Safety Governance Framework 2.0 — The issuers' own English title on the PDF cover is 'AI Safety Governance Framework 2.0'.
- **CN-13** original_title: 人工智能安全治理框架（2.0版） → 人工智能安全治理框架2.0 — Taken verbatim from the PDF cover (人工智能 / 安全治理框架2.0); the earlier form with parentheses does not occur in the stored texts.
- **CN-13** language: Chinese → Chinese, English — The PDF carries the issuers' English text after the Chinese.
- **CN-13** summary: Revised version of the national AI safety governance framew… → Version 2.0 of China's national AI safety governance framew… — Rewritten from the Chinese framework text; the earlier summary was under 250 characters and added 'technical' to 防范治理措施.
- **CN-13** safety_topics: + catastrophic risk, + human oversight, + international cooperation — Added catastrophic risk (section 3, Annex 1), human oversight (principle 1.5, Annex 2) and international cooperation (5.14), all in the text.
- **CN-13** notes: Released at the main forum of the 2025 National Cybersecuri… → Released on 15 Sept 2025 at the main forum of the 2025 Nati… — Added the governance measures on critical information infrastructure, classification standards, evaluation and loss-of-control testing from the text.
- **CN-13** english_version: - → {"kind": "authentic", "url": "https://www.cac.gov.cn/rootim… — Authentic English text in the issuers' own PDF, a stored source.
- **CN-16** summary: Regulates AI services that simulate human personality and g… → Regulates AI services that simulate a natural person's pers… — The scope now follows Art. 2: continuing emotional interaction (持续性的情感互动服务), not 'emotional companionship', which is only one of the services Art. 2 lists (care, companionship, support). The ban reads as in Art. 14, on virtual intimate-relationship services such as virtual relatives or partners.
